2026-07-30/07-31 (BR-121 foundation + 4 of 5 IDOR stopgaps: checklistEquipmentMaster, keyIngredientsMaster, storewiseGenericMapper merged; departmentNursingStationMapper fixed + fully live-verified, held for approval) | Maker + Checker (self) | Claude Code (Sonnet 5) | main (BR-121/122/123/124, merged) + fix/departmentnursingstationmapper-crosstenant-idor (held, not merged) | Root-caused a foundational gap discovered this session: `organizationId` is never cryptographically verified anywhere in the app -- the JWT only signs `unitId`; every existing "trusted" org source (including `SessionService.getOrganizationId()`, used by ~138 files) reads an unsigned, client-editable `cookieObject` cookie. Fixed the foundation first (**BR-121**): a new stateless `VerifiedIdentityService.getVerifiedOrganizationId()` derives `organizationId` server-side from the JWT-verified `unitId` via a single `m_unit_master` lookup, never trusting any client-supplied source. Applied it as a stopgap fix to 4 of the 5 screens found vulnerable to cross-tenant IDOR this session, each on its own dedicated branch with full live before/after verification (seeded victim tenant `organization_id=999`/`unit_id=999`, forged requests reproduced pre-fix and blocked post-fix, legitimate same-tenant requests re-confirmed unaffected, DB state cross-checked via `psql` after every test and restored): **BR-122** `checklistEquipmentMaster` (update/status-toggle had zero ownership check at the DAO level even after a DTO-level org/unit overwrite), **BR-123** `keyIngredientsMaster` (list/getActive queries' `unitId` filter was a dangling comment outside the query string, not just unused -- never actually applied), **BR-124** `storewiseGenericMapper` (broadest of the three -- `getById` had zero scoping at all, `getAll`/search trusted URL-path values, plus an independent unparenthesized-`OR` bug in the search HQL that let any tenant's row bypass the unit/org filter by matching on code alone). All three merged into `main`, full multi-module Java build verified clean after each merge. **departmentNursingStationMapper (BR-121 stopgap #4, provisionally BR-125, not yet recorded in the manifest -- see below) fixed and fully live-verified but deliberately NOT merged this entry:** same pattern -- every one of its 10 endpoints trusted client-supplied `organisationId`/`unitId` (URL path or request body) with zero server-side check, plus an independent bug in `GET_WARD_LIST_BY_NURSING_STATION` (a `t_nursing_station_ward_mapper` native query) that had no `organization_id`/`unit_id` predicate at all, leaking cross-tenant ward IDs by `nursingStationId` alone regardless of what org/unit the caller supplied. Fixed by injecting `VerifiedIdentityService` into `DepartmentNursingStationMapperServiceImpl` (overriding org/unit on every method) and restoring the missing predicate on the native query, matching the already-correct sibling query `GET_WARD_LIST_BY_USER_ID`. Verified live, full matrix, before and after: a forged `getActiveDepartmentNursingStationMapper/999/999` and `getDepartmentNursingStationMapperCount/999/999` (real org1/unit1 admin session) returned the victim tenant's data pre-fix, rescoped to the real tenant post-fix; a forged `getDeptListByNursingStationId/4/1/1` (victim's `nursingStationId`, attacker's own real org/unit in the URL) leaked the victim's ward id pre-fix, returned empty post-fix; most notably, a forged cross-tenant **write** (`updateDepartmentNursingStationMapperStatus/1/999/999/I`, called by the real org1/unit1 admin) actually flipped the victim's row from `'A'` to `'I'` pre-fix -- confirmed via `psql` -- and post-fix the identical request instead flips the real tenant's own row, victim row confirmed untouched. DB state restored via `psql` after each test. Full multi-module Java reactor build (`mvn compile`, all 18 modules) verified clean with the fix applied. Held on `fix/departmentnursingstationmapper-crosstenant-idor`, not merged -- awaiting explicit owner approval per standing discipline; the manifest/`BR_INDEX.md` entry will be recorded (as BR-125 or whatever the next free number is at merge time) only once merged, matching the established BR-122/123/124 pattern of recording after, not before, approval. departmentNursingStationMapper's separate zero-count-as-error pattern and 3 other correctness bugs (found during this same investigation, logged in `docs/ANGULAR_PENDING_FINDINGS.md`, commit `4a690c8a`) remain deliberately unfixed, out of scope for this IDOR stopgap, owner-decided to defer to the eventual Angular rebuild. Remaining work: `userNursingStationMapper`, the 5th and final screen from this session's original IDOR sweep, not yet started; a known correction still owed to BR-115 (`pharmacyproductCategoryMaster`, fixed earlier this session against the spoofable `SessionService` value, not yet re-verified against the new `VerifiedIdentityService` foundation -- flagged, not yet actioned).
